        {
            n3++;
            int x = (int)r.Next(dgBounds.L, dgBounds.R);
            int y = (int)r.Next(worldSurface + 25, dgBounds.B);
            if (wal[y * W + x] == num && act[y * W + x] == 0)
            {
                int d = 1;
                if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0) d = -1;
                int wx = x, wy = y;
                while (DgInWorld(wx, wy, 0) && act[wy * W + wx] == 0)
                {
                    if (round == 0) wy += d; else wx += d;
                }
                bool hit = DgInWorld(wx, wy, 0);
                bool okA, okB, okC, okD2, okE;
                if (round == 0)
                {
                    okA = hit && DgInWorld(wx - 1, wy, 0) && act[wy * W + wx - 1] != 0;
                    okB = hit && DgInWorld(wx + 1, wy, 0) && act[wy * W + wx + 1] != 0;
                    okC = hit && DgInWorld(wx - 1, wy, 0) && DgCanSupportSpike(wx - 1, wy);
                    okD2 = hit && DgInWorld(wx - 1, wy - d, 0) && act[(wy - d) * W + wx - 1] == 0;
                    okE = hit && DgInWorld(wx + 1, wy - d, 0) && act[(wy - d) * W + wx + 1] == 0;
                }
                else
                {
                    okA = hit && DgInWorld(wx, wy - 1, 0) && act[(wy - 1) * W + wx] != 0;
                    okB = hit && DgInWorld(wx, wy + 1, 0) && act[(wy + 1) * W + wx] != 0;
                    okC = hit && DgInWorld(wx, wy - 1, 0) && DgCanSupportSpike(wx, wy - 1);
                    okD2 = hit && DgInWorld(wx - d, wy - 1, 0) && act[(wy - 1) * W + wx - d] == 0;
                    okE = hit && DgInWorld(wx - d, wy + 1, 0) && act[(wy + 1) * W + wx - d] == 0;
                }
                if (okA && okB && okC && okD2 && okE)
                {
                    n5++;
                    // ★D8：两段起点对齐原版（DungeonGlobalSpikes.cs:66-72/:163-170）——命中判定后
                    // 不预偏移，首刺落在命中格本身，while 条件查"步进方向下一格"支撑、放置当前格
                    //（:73-96/:170-193）。曾先 -1 使整段尖刺列整体错位一格、丢命中格尖刺。
                    // 第一段：沿主轴负向刻尖刺（起点=命中格）
                    int n11 = (int)r.Next(5, 13);
                    int cx = wx, cy = wy;
                    DgSpikeRun(round, ref cx, ref cy, wx, wy, d, ref n11, step: round == 0 ? -1 : 0, decY: round != 0);
                    // 第二段：沿主轴正向刻尖刺（round0 起点=原始采样 x+1 即 cs:105 num9+1；
                    // round1 起点=原始采样 y+1 即 cs:190 num13+1，非命中格 wy+1）
                    n11 = (int)r.Next(5, 13);
                    cx = round == 0 ? x + 1 : wx; cy = round == 0 ? wy : y + 1;
                    DgSpikeRun(round, ref cx, ref cy, wx, wy, d, ref n11, step: round == 0 ? 1 : 0, decY: round != 0);
                }
            }
            if (n3 > n4) { n3 = 0; n5++; }
        }
    }
}
// 单段尖刺刻划（DungeonGlobalSpikes.cs:950-999 round0 / :1047-1096 round1）
void DgSpikeRun(int round, ref int cx, ref int cy, int wx, int wy, int d, ref int n11, int step, bool decY)
{
    while (n11 > 0)
    {
        bool cond;
        if (round == 0)
        {
            int nb = cx + step;
            cond = DgInWorld(nb, wy, 0) && act[wy * W + nb] != 0 && DgCanSupportSpike(nb, wy)
                && DgInWorld(cx, wy + d, 0) && act[(wy + d) * W + cx] != 0
                && DgInWorld(cx, wy, 0) && act[wy * W + cx] != 0
                && DgInWorld(cx, wy - d, 0) && act[(wy - d) * W + cx] == 0;
        }
        else
        {
            int nb = cy + step;
            cond = DgInWorld(wx, nb, 0) && act[nb * W + wx] != 0 && DgCanSupportSpike(wx, nb)
                && DgInWorld(wx + d, cy, 0) && act[cy * W + wx + d] != 0
                && DgInWorld(wx, cy, 0) && act[cy * W + wx] != 0
                && DgInWorld(wx - d, cy, 0) && act[cy * W + wx - d] == 0;
        }
        if (!cond) break;
        int fx = round == 0 ? cx : wx, fy = round == 0 ? wy : cy;
        if (!DgFeatureOK(fx, fy, 0) || !DgFeatureOK(fx, round == 0 ? wy - d : cy - d, 0))
        {
            if (round == 0) cx += step; else cy += step;
            n11 = 0;
            continue;
        }
        typ[fy * W + fx] = 48;
        if (round == 0)
        {
            if (DgInWorld(cx - 1, wy - d, 0) && act[(wy - d) * W + cx - 1] == 0
                && DgInWorld(cx + 1, wy - d, 0) && act[(wy - d) * W + cx + 1] == 0)
            {
                int t2 = (wy - d) * W + cx; typ[t2] = 48; act[t2] = 1;
                int t3 = (wy - d * 2) * W + cx; typ[t3] = 48; act[t3] = 1;
            }
            cx += step;
        }
        else
        {
            if (DgInWorld(wx - d, cy - 1, 0) && act[(cy - 1) * W + wx - d] == 0
                && DgInWorld(wx - d, cy + 1, 0) && act[(cy + 1) * W + wx - d] == 0)
            {
                int t2 = cy * W + wx - d; typ[t2] = 48; act[t2] = 1;
                int t3 = cy * W + wx - d * 2; typ[t3] = 48; act[t3] = 1;
            }
            cy += step;
        }
        n11--;
    }
}
// DungeonData.CanGenerateFeatureAt（DungeonData.cs:132-166；pit 陷阱仅放行 Paintings/WallVariants；
// 入口 Bounds 仅拦 BiomeChests）。feat: 0=常规特征 1=BiomeChests 2=Paintings/WallVariants
bool DgFeatureOK(int x, int y, int feat)
{
    if (!DgInWorld(x, y, 5)) return false;
    if (wal[y * W + x] == 350) return false;
    if (feat == 1 && dgEntExists && dgEntBounds.Contains(x, y)) return false;
    if (feat != 2)
        for (int i = 0; i < dgPits.Count; i++)
            if (dgPits[i].Contains(x, y)) return false;
    return true;
}
bool DgFeatureAt(int x, int y, bool allowPW) => DgFeatureOK(x, y, allowPW ? 2 : 0);
// —— DungeonGlobalDoors（:24-294）——
bool DgCanKill(int x, int y)
{
    if (!DgInWorld(x, y, 0)) return false;
    int ti = y * W + x;
    if (act[ti] == 0) return false;
    if (wal[ti] == 350) return false;
    ushort t = typ[ti];
    if (t == 10) return false;                                 // IsLockedDoor(style13)=true
    if (t == 21 || t == 467 || t == 88) return false;          // CanDestroyChest：生成期箱内有物
    return true;
}
void DgDoorsF()

230		private bool Spikes_CanSupportSpike(int x, int y)
231		{
232			Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
233			if (!tile.active())
234			{
235				return false;
236			}
237			if (tile.type >= 0 && (Main.tileFrameImportant[tile.type] || Main.tileCut[tile.type]))
238			{
239				return false;
240			}
241			if (DungeonUtils.IsConsideredCrackedDungeonTile(tile.type))
242			{
243				return false;
244			}
245			return true;
246		}
247	}

V_ID[st.type[i]] : 0;   // 幽灵 type 归零（同 oracle typ 落盘口径? 见下）\n      typ.writeUInt16LE(Math.max(0, v), i * 2);\n      act[i] = st.flags[i] ? 49 : 48;                 // \"1\"/\"0\"\n    }\n    writeFileSync(`/tmp/js-${name}.bin`, typ);\n    writeFileSync(`/tmp/js-${name}.act`, act.toString('latin1'));\n    console.log(`dumped ${name}`);\n  };\n  runDunesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runOceanSandPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runSandPatchesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runTunnelsPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runMountCavesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runDirtWallBgPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runRocksAndClayPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runSmallHolesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runCavesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runIceBiomePass(st, rng, gs);\n  runGrassPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runJunglePass(st, rng, gs);\n  runMudCavesPass(st, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!);\n  const desc = runDesertMoundOnly(st, rng, gs);\n  runDesertEntrancesAndHive(st, rng, gs, desc, () => {});\n  runMushroomPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runMarblePass(st, rng, gs);\n  runGranitePass(st, rng, gs);\n  runFloatingIslandsPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runDirtToMudPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runSiltPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runShiniesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runWebsPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runUnderworldPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runCorruptionPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runLakesPass(st, rng, gs);\n  runSlushPass(st, gs);\n  runDungeonPass(st, rng, gs, world.chests, (name) => {\n    if (name === 'dungeonC') dump('dgC');\n    if (name === 'dungeonB') dump('dgB');\n  });\n}, 300000);\n"

{
 "command": "python3 - <<'EOF'\nW=4200\ndef load(t,a): return open(t,'rb').read(), open(a,'rb').read()\ndef u16(b,i): return b[i*2]|(b[i*2+1]<<8)\njC,jCa=load('/tmp/js-dgC.bin','/tmp/js-dgC.act'); jB,jBa=load('/tmp/js-dgB.bin','/tmp/js-dgB.act')\noC,oCa=load('/tmp/oracle-dgC.bin','/tmp/oracle-dgC.bin.act'); oB,oBa=load('/tmp/oracle-dgB.bin','/tmp/oracle-dgB.bin.act')\ndef changed(tC,aC,tB,aB):\n    out=[]\n    for i in range(W*1200):\n        c=(u16(tC,i),aC[i]); b=(u16(tB,i),aB[i])\n        if c!=b: out.append((i%W,i//W,c,b))\n    return out\ncj=changed(jC,jCa,jB,jBa); co=changed(oC,oCa,oB,oBa)\ndef clusters(cells):\n    cells_set={(x,y) for x,y,_,_ in cells}\n    seen=set(); out=[]\n    for x,y,c,b in cells:\n        if (x,y) in seen: continue\n        stack=[(x,y)]; comp=[]\n        seen.add((x,y))\n        while stack:\n            cx,cy=stack.pop(); comp.append((cx,cy))\n            for dx in(-1,0,1):\n                for dy in(-1,0,1):\n                    nx,ny=cx+dx,cy+dy\n                    if (nx,ny) in cells_set and (nx,ny) not in seen:\n                        seen.add((nx,ny)); stack.append((nx,ny))\n        out.append(comp)\n    return out\ndef summarize(name, cells):\n    print(f'--- {name}: {len(cells)} changed, {len(clusters(cells))} clusters ---')\n    for comp in clusters(cells):\n        info={ (x,y): next((f'{c[0]}{\"+\" if c[1]==49 else \"-\"}→{b[0]}{\"+\" if b[1]==49 else \"-\"}' for xx,yy,c,b in cells if xx==x and yy==y)) for x,y in comp }\n        xs=[c[0] for c in comp]; ys=[c[1] for c in comp]\n        # compact: group by y\n        rows={}\n        for (x,y),v in info.items(): rows.setdefault(y,[]).append((x,v))\n        desc='; '.join(f'y{y}:' + ','.join(f'x{x}={v}' for x,v in sorted(rows[y].items() if False else rows[y])) for y in sorted(rows))\n        print(f'  cluster bbox x[{min(xs)}-{max(xs)}] y[{min(ys)}-{max(ys)}] n={len(comp)}: {desc}')\nsummarize('JS', cj)\nsummarize('ORACLE', co)\nEOF",
 "description": "Cluster and summarize all changed cells per side",
 "timeout": 180000
}

Root cause #2 confirmed: `TILE_BY_KEY['v_13_bottles']` is `undefined` — the correct key is `v_13_placed_bottles` (sheet 13). Two effects:
1. Bottles place as type 0 (undefined coerced) with flags=1 — wrong content.
2. The follow-up `rn(2)` (vanilla's frameX 18/36 draw when placed type==13) never fires → draw stream under-consumption vs vanilla/oracle → everything downstream diverges.

Also note line 1740's guard `typAt(x, y) === TILE_BY_KEY['v_13_bottles']` is always false — dead check.

Fix in DungeonPass.ts:
- line 1739: `TILE_BY_KEY['v_13_bottles']` → `TILE_BY_KEY['v_13_placed_bottles']!`
- line 1740: same replacement.

Wait — but hold on: is placing bottles with placeSimple correct vs vanilla? Vanilla: `WorldGen.PlaceTile(num5, num6, num12, mute: true)` — places 13 or 49. Oracle: DgPlaceSimple(x, y, n12). JS: placeSimple ✓ same approximation as oracle. Good.

Also — `WATER_CANDLE = TILE_BY_KEY['water_candle']!` → sheet 49 ✓ correct. BOOKS = v_50_books sheet 50 ✓.

Now, one more check on the `!flag3` branch — vanilla for DEFAULT dungeon (flag2=true): num12 = Next(4)==0 ? 1 : 0 → map 0→13, 1→49. JS: `rn(4) === 0 ? 49 : 13` ✓ same distribution. Oracle: `n12 = Next(4)==0 ? 1 : 0; n12 = n12==0 ? 13 : 49` ✓ same.

And the draw semantics: vanilla consumes Next(2) if `Main.tile[num5, num6].type == 13` — i.e., if the PLACED tile is bottles — note this fires even if PlaceTile FAILED but the cell already contained type 13? (edge case, irrelevant). Oracle: `if (DgInWorld && typ==13) r.Next(2)` — typ AFTER DgPlaceSimple. If DgPlaceSimple failed, typ = whatever was there (0/other) → no draw. JS with the fixed key: `typAt(x,y) === v_13_placed_bottles` → same semantics as oracle. ✓

So fixing the key fixes both content and stream. Let me also check for OTHER uses of the wrong key in this file or elsewhere — grep showed only lines 1739/1740 use 'v_13_bottles'. Let me double check across src.

bool DgFeatArea3(int x, int y, int fluff)
{
    for (int a = x - fluff; a <= x + fluff; a++)
        for (int b = y - fluff; b <= y + fluff; b++)
            if (!DgFeatureOK(a, b, 2)) return false;
    return true;
}
bool DgNearPicture(int x, int y)
{
    for (int a = x - 4; a <= x + 3; a++)
        for (int b = y - 3; b <= y + 2; b++)
            if (DgInWorld(a, b, 0) && act[b * W + a] != 0) return true;
    return false;
}
bool DgNearPicture2(int x, int y)
{
    int rad1 = (wal[y * W + x] != 7 && wal[y * W + x] != 8 && wal[y * W + x] != 9) ? 8 : 15;
    int rad2 = (wal[y * W + x] != 7 && wal[y * W + x] != 8 && wal[y * W + x] != 9) ? 5 : 10;
    for (int a = x - rad1; a <= x + rad1; a++)
        for (int b = y - rad2; b <= y + rad2; b++)
            if (DgInWorld(a, b, 0) && act[b * W + a] != 0 && (typ[b * W + a] == 240 || typ[b * W + a] == 241 || typ[b * W + a] == 242)) return true;
    return false;
}

```
	public bool CanGenerateFeatureInArea(IDungeonFeature feature, int x, int y, int fluff)
	{
		DungeonBounds dungeonBounds = new DungeonBounds();
		dungeonBounds.SetBounds(x - fluff, y - fluff, x + fluff, y + fluff);
		dungeonBounds.CalculateHitbox();
		return CanGenerateFeatureInArea(feature, dungeonBounds);
	}

	public bool CanGenerateFeatureInArea(IDungeonFeature feature, DungeonBounds bounds)
	{
		if (!bounds.HasHitbox())
		{
			return false;
		}
		return CanGenerateFeatureInArea(feature, bounds.Hitbox);
	}

	public bool CanGenerateFeatureInArea(IDungeonFeature feature, Rectangle hitbox)
	{
		for (int i = hitbox.Left; i <= hitbox.Right; i++)
		{
			for (int j = hitbox.Top; j <= hitbox.Bottom; j++)
			{
				if (!CanGenerateFeatureAt(feature, i, j))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

```

}
Checkpoint("beaches");
//   本行与 mcopenings 块之间（原版真实位 29/32）。

// ================= RNG 流位置指纹（UnifiedRandom 内部态哈希）=================
// 对账用探针：与逐 pass 网格哈希正交——若本指纹一致而网格哈希分叉，分歧是
// 「写格内容」而非「掷骰数」；两者皆分叉则是流已分叉（上游 pass 掷骰数不同）。
// JS 侧对应 tests/shimmer-checkpoint.test.ts 的 streamHash()（同字节序同算法）。
var streamPts = new List<string>();
uint StreamHash()
{
    var t = asm.GetType("Terraria.Utilities.UnifiedRandom")!;
    var sa = (int[])t.GetField("SeedArray", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!.GetValue(r)!;
    var ix = (uint)t.GetField("inext", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!.GetValue(r)!;
    uint h = 0x811c9dc5;
    foreach (var v in sa) { for (int b = 0; b < 4; b++) h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> (8 * b)) & 0xff)) * 16777619u; }
    for (int b = 0; b < 4; b++) h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> (8 * b)) & 0xff)) * 16777619u;
    return h;
}
void StreamPt(string n) => streamPts.Add($"{n}|{StreamHash():x8}");
StreamPt("beaches");

function streamHash(rng: RNG): string {
  const ur = (rng as unknown as { ur: { seedArray: Int32Array; inext: number } }).ur;
  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
  const step = (b: number) => { h = Math.imul(h ^ (b & 0xff), 16777619) >>> 0; };
  for (let i = 0; i < ur.seedArray.length; i++) {
    const v = ur.seedArray[i] | 0;
    for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) step(v >>> (8 * b));
  }
  const ix = ur.inext >>> 0;
  for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) step(ix >>> (8 * b));
  return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

/** 微光链检查点（金标 + JS 同名落点）。

function shimmerChain(seedText: string, evil: 0 | 1) {
  const { world, rng, gs } = runResetAndTerrain({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText, evil });
  const st = world.store;
  const grids = new Map<string, string>();

}
